HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_063fe71767ffbc3d099c","text":"1. **A repeatable operating rule, not expendable restatement.** Fixed `ع س ر/B001` and `ي س ر/B001` again supply difficulty and ready opening. Unlike 94:5, this occurrence is not introduced by the initial **ف**, and its verbatim root sequence immediately precedes the conditional work command in 94:7. Repetition gives the relation persistence; the ease branch gives readiness for use. **Abductive move:** I infer first occurrence as inference from prior events and second occurrence as a reusable rule that licenses the next transition; that discourse function is not explicitly labeled. **Reading change:** 94:6 stabilizes the relation for future cycles instead of merely intensifying the preceding sentence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B001","root_001694/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_09fc597d13a579b0fc4f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:6:3\",\"root_001012\",[\"B001\"]],[\"94:6:4\",\"root_001694\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Tekrarın kendisi, daha önce yükseltilen anılmayı söz içinde uygulayarak zorluk ve kolaylık bağını unutulmaya karşı sürekli hazır tutar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B005","root_001694/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0adda92db881b06ba65f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:6:3\",\"root_001012\",[\"B005\"]],[\"94:6:4\",\"root_001694\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Zorluk ve kolaylığın ortak sol taraf çağrışımı, karşıtlık yerine aynı güçlük içinde birlikte bulunan iki farklı kullanım biçimini öne sürebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2943b0cd269df3acb53f","text":"2. **From entanglement to maneuverability.** Fixed `ع س ر/B004` is opposition, complication, or entanglement, while fixed `ي س ر/B005` is light, pliant, quickly responsive movement. In 94:7, `ف ر غ/B003` adds breadth of movement and `ن ص ب/B010` adds sustained travel; 94:8 directs that motion. The hardship branch supplies constrained movement and the ease branch supplies regained handling. **Abductive move:** I infer ease as the release of mechanical entanglement that permits reorientation and travel. **Reading change:** the paired nouns describe recoverable maneuverability, not a static contrast between bad and good conditions.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3b794350f3447367db04","text":"The later ayah that most changed 94:6 was 94:7: `فَإِذَا فَرَغْتَ فَٱنصَبْ` made the second ease statement feel like a hinge into action. The repetition is not only reassurance; it prepares a person to stand up under a new assigned portion or strain. 94:8 then made the measured-share reading more coherent, because `ر ب ب B010/B011` gathers lots/covenant while `ر غ ب B004` seeks bounty; ease becomes a provision within directed dependence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B004","root_001694/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3d3bd32d0b793cadcf43","text":"94:7 was the decisive correction: it made `ي س ر/B001` readiness for another exertion rather than release from exertion altogether. Its motion branches, followed by the direction in 94:8, also exposed the latent `ع س ر/B004` entanglement versus `ي س ر/B005` maneuverability mechanism.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B001","root_001694/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4450d8a5295839a5c7e7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:6:3\",\"root_001012\",[\"B001\"]],[\"94:6:4\",\"root_001694\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Aynı ilişkinin ikinci kez söylenmesi, önceki olaylardan çıkarılan sonucu gelecek zorluklarda da işletilebilen kalıcı bir kurala dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B001","root_001694/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b9f4f65eabf013661b77","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:6:3\",\"root_001012\",[\"B001\"]],[\"94:6:4\",\"root_001694\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Paylaştırılmış hisse çağrışımı, kolaylığın sınırsız bolluk değil, her zorluk döneminin içinde ayrılan ölçülü bir imkân olduğunu düşündürebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001694/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c2605a1d3db2d4123154","text":"3. **Ease as readiness for effort, not vacancy from effort.** Fixed `ي س ر/B001` includes becoming ready or making ready, while fixed `ع س ر/B001` marks the resistant condition. The very next ayah joins `ف ر غ/B001` completion or vacancy to `ن ص ب/B004` toil. The fixed ease branch supplies preparedness; 94:7 supplies what that preparedness is for. **Abductive move:** I infer ease -> capacity for renewed exertion rather than ease -> permanent inactivity. **Reading change:** this second assertion corrects a possible reading of ease as an endpoint and recasts it as usable readiness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B004","root_001694/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d4a3a73e837f81c6c13c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:6:3\",\"root_001012\",[\"B004\"]],[\"94:6:4\",\"root_001694\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Düğümlenme ile hafif ve uysal hareket çağrışımları, kolaylığı durağan rahatlıktan çok kısıt içinde yeniden yön değiştirebilme yeteneğine dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B001","root_001694/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d69e6f7ba283684e690e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:6:3\",\"root_001012\",[\"B001\"]],[\"94:6:4\",\"root_001694\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hemen ardından gelen çalışma buyruğu, bu ikinci kolaylığı çabanın sona ermesi değil, yeni bir çabaya hazır olma durumu olarak sabitler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B001","root_001012/B004","root_001694/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e16598cb40333d0607d6","text":"1. The repeated line shifts the pattern from consequence to durable rule. Lexical evidence: the same `ع س ر B001` difficulty and `ي س ر B001` ease/readiness recur. Structural cue: 94:5 begins with `فَإِنَّ`, while 94:6 repeats without the initial `فـ`. Assigned role: the first occurrence follows from the preceding acts; the second reasserts the relation as stable in itself. Abductive move supplied by me: repetition changes rhetorical force from inference to anchoring. Reading change: the ayah reads as a second fastening of the ease-hardship relation. 2. The repetition itself behaves like raised memory. Lexical evidence: `ذ ك ر B003/B009` recalling and reminder; `ر ف ع B005` announcing a report. Structural cue: 94:4 raises `ذكر`, and 94:5-6 immediately repeat the saying. Assigned role: the doubled sentence is the content being kept present. Abductive move supplied by me: the raised mention expresses itself as repeated remembrance. Reading change: 94:6 reads not only as semantic repetition but as enacted remembrance. 3. The paired lines enact coexistence rather than sequence. Lexical evidence: `ع س ر B004` entanglement/making difficult, `ي س ر B005` pliant compliant movement, and the shared left-side branches `ع س ر B005` / `ي س ر B004`. Structural cue: the exact pair `العسر / يسرا` is held together twice by `مع`. Assigned role: hardship is an entangling field; ease is the pliant movement possible within it. Abductive move supplied by me: repeated co-presence makes coexistence more salient than before/after progression. Reading change: the ayah becomes training in movement under constraint. 4. A measured-share reading is activated from later `نصب`. Lexical evidence: `ي س ر B007` lots and apportioned camel shares; later `ن ص ب B005` assigned share/portion and `ن ص ب B006` fixed threshold; `ر ب ب B010` container gathering arrows/lots. Structural cue: the later imperative `فانصب` introduces allotted portion/base vocabulary, and final `ربك` includes a branch for gathered lots. Assigned role: ease may be a distributed share within hardship, not total abundance. Abductive move supplied by me: the repeated indefinite `يسرا` can be heard as apportioned openings assigned within one named hardship. Reading change: 94:6 reads as measured provision, not unlimited relief.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
